      </xsl:choose>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="count($ndCur/*) &gt; 1 and contains($OpenChars, $sNdText)">
        <xsl:value-of select="$sNdText" />
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>
        <xsl:value-of select="''" />
      </xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- %%Template: determines if the text in the last child of ndCur is a fence close character
       and there is more than 1 child. Also checks if the last child is an mrow containing
       a single mo with a fence close character (as generated by MathJax and WikiMedia).
       This includes all characters used as a close bracket in the Brackets Gallery in Office.
  -->
  <xsl:template name="FenceCloseChar">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:variable name="sNdText">
      <xsl:choose>
        <xsl:when test="$ndCur/*[last()][self::mml:mo]">
          <xsl:value-of select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[last()])" />
        </xsl:when>
        <xsl:when test="$ndCur/*[last()][self::mml:mrow[count(*)=1 and mml:mo]]">
          <xsl:value-of select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[last()]/mml:mo)" />
        </xsl:when>
      </xsl:choose>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="count($ndCur/*) &gt; 1 and contains($CloseChars, $sNdText)">
        <xsl:value-of select="$sNdText" />
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>
        <xsl:value-of select="''" />
      </xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- %%Template: determines whether the fenced set, ndCur, has a matching separator
       character for all mo child elements that aren't the fist or last child. If there
       is a matching character, return it, otherwise return an empty string.
  -->
  <xsl:template name="FenceSeparatorChar">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:variable name="chPotentialSeparator">
      <xsl:value-of select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[not(position()=1 or position()=last())][self::mml:mo][1])" />
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="count($ndCur/*[not(position()=1 or position()=last())][self::mml:mo]) > 0 and
                      count($ndCur/*[not(position()=1 or position()=last())][self::mml:mo])=
                      count($ndCur/*[not(position()=1 or position()=last())][self::mml:mo][normalize-space(self::mml:mo)=$chPotentialSeparator])">
        <xsl:value-of select="$chPotentialSeparator" />
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>
        <xsl:value-of select="''" />
      </xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- %%Template: determines whether ndCur fits the pattern of a fenced set.
       1.  It is an mrow
       2.  The first and last child are mo elements with a fence open and matching match close charater or
           The first child is an mo with a fence open character and the last child is not an mo with a fence close character
             and there are no mo elements with the matching close character or
           The last child is an mo with a fence close character and the first child is not an mo with a fence open character
             and there are no mo elements with the matching open character
       This covers all items in the Brackets Gallery in Office.
  -->
  <xsl:template name="FFenced">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:variable name="fenceOpenChar">
      <xsl:call-template name="FenceOpenChar">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:variable name="fenceCloseChar">
      <xsl:call-template name="FenceCloseChar">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:variable name="matchingCloseCharForOpenChar">
      <xsl:value-of select="translate($fenceOpenChar, $OpenChars, $CloseChars)" />
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:variable name="matchingOpenCharForCloseChar">
      <xsl:value-of select="translate($fenceCloseChar, $CloseChars, $OpenChars)" />
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur/self::mml:mrow and
                      (($fenceOpenChar!='' and $fenceCloseChar!='' and
                        $fenceCloseChar=$matchingCloseCharForOpenChar) or
                        ($fenceOpenChar!='' and $fenceCloseChar='' and
                        not($ndCur/mml:mo[normalize-space(.)=$matchingCloseCharForOpenChar])) or
                        ($fenceOpenChar='' and $fenceCloseChar!='' and
                        not($ndCur/mml:mo[normalize-space(.)=$matchingOpenCharForCloseChar])))">1</xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- %%Template: determines if ndCur is a fence operator element
  -->
  <xsl:template name="FFenceOperator">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:variable name="fFencedParent">
      <xsl:call-template name="FFenced">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur/.." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:variable name="fenceOpenChar">
      <xsl:call-template name="FenceOpenChar">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur/.." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:variable name="fenceCloseChar">
      <xsl:call-template name="FenceCloseChar">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur/.." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:variable name="fenceSeparatorChar">
      <xsl:call-template name="FenceSeparatorChar">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur/.." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="$fFencedParent = 1 and
                      (($ndCur/parent::*/*[1] and $fenceOpenChar!='') or
                        ($ndCur/parent::*/*[last()] and $fenceCloseChar!='') or
                        (not($ndCur = $ndCur/parent::*/*[1]) and
                          not($ndCur = $ndCur/parent::*/*[last()]) and
                          $fenceSeparatorChar!=''))">1</xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- %%Template: determines whether ndCur fits the pattern of a fenced set where the closing
       fence character is the base (first child) of a script element (msup, msub, msubsup,
       munder, mover, munderover) that is the last child of the mrow.
       This pattern is generated by KaTeX for expressions like (x)^2, where the closing paren
       is placed inside the msup element as:
         <mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow>
  -->
  <xsl:template name="FFencedWithScript">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:variable name="fenceOpenChar">
      <xsl:call-template name="FenceOpenChar">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:variable name="matchingCloseChar">
      <xsl:value-of select="translate($fenceOpenChar, $OpenChars, $CloseChars)" />
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:variable name="lastChildBaseText" select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[last()]/*[1][self::mml:mo])" />
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur/self::mml:mrow and
                      $fenceOpenChar != '' and
                      $ndCur/*[last()][self::mml:msup or self::mml:msub or self::mml:msubsup or
                                       self::mml:munder or self::mml:mover or self::mml:munderover] and
                      $lastChildBaseText != '' and
                      contains($CloseChars, $lastChildBaseText) and
                      $lastChildBaseText = $matchingCloseChar">1</xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- Though presentation mathml can certainly typeset any generic function with the
       appropriate function operator spacing, presentation MathML has no concept of
       a function structure like omml does.  In order to preserve the omml <func>
       element, we must establish how an omml <func> element looks in mml.  This
       is shown below:
       
       <mml:mrow>
         <mml:mrow>
            // function name
         </mml:mrow>
         <mml:mo>&#x02061;</mml:mo>
         <mml:mrow>
            // function argument
         </mml:mrow>
       </mml:mrow>
       
       This template looks for four things to be true:
          1.  ndCur has three children
          2.  ndCur is an mml:mrow
          3.  The second child is an mml:mo
          4.  The second child's text is '&#x02061;'
       -->
  <xsl:template name="FIsFunc">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:variable name="sNdText">
      <xsl:value-of select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[2])" />
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:choose>
      <!-- Is this an omml function -->
      <xsl:when test="count($ndCur/*)=3 and
                      $ndCur/self::*[self::mml:mrow] and
                      $ndCur/*[2][self::mml:mo] and
                      $sNdText='&#x02061;'">1</xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- Given the node of the linear fraction's parent mrow, 
       make a linear fraction -->
  <xsl:template name="MakeLinearFraction">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:element name="m:f">
      <xsl:element name="m:fPr">
        <xsl:element name="m:type">
          <xsl:attribute name="m:val">lin</xsl:attribute>
        </xsl:element>
      </xsl:element>
      <xsl:element name="m:num">
        <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
        <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
      </xsl:element>
      <xsl:element name="m:den">
        <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
        <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[3]" />
      </xsl:element>
    </xsl:element>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- Given the node of the function's parent mrow,
       make an omml function -->
  <xsl:template name="WriteFunc">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />

    <xsl:element name="m:func">
      <xsl:element name="m:fName">
        <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/child::*[1]" />
      </xsl:element>
      <xsl:element name="m:e">
        <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/child::*[3]" />
      </xsl:element>
    </xsl:element>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- MathML doesn't have the concept of nAry structures.  The best approximation
       to these is to have some under/over or sub/sup followed by an mrow or mstyle.
       
       In the case that we've come across some under/over or sub/sup that contains an
       nAry operator, this function handles the following sibling to the nAry structure.
       
       If the following sibling is:
       
          mml:mstyle, then apply templates to the children of this mml:mstyle
          
          mml:mrow, determine if this mrow is a linear fraction
          (see comments for FlinearFrac template).
              If so, make an Omml linear fraction.
              If not, apply templates as was done for mml:mstyle.
       
       -->
  <xsl:template name="NaryHandleMrowMstyle">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <!-- if the next sibling is an mrow, pull it in by
         doing whatever we would have done to its children.
         The mrow itself will be skipped, see template above. -->
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:mrow]">
        <!-- Check for linear fraction -->
        <xsl:variable name="fLinearFrac">
          <xsl:call-template name="FLinearFrac">
            <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
          </xsl:call-template>
        </xsl:variable>
        <xsl:choose>
          <xsl:when test="$fLinearFrac=1">
            <xsl:call-template name="MakeLinearFraction">
              <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
            </xsl:call-template>
          </xsl:when>
          <xsl:otherwise>
            <xsl:variable name="fFunc">
              <xsl:call-template name="FIsFunc">
                <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
              </xsl:call-template>
            </xsl:variable>
            <xsl:choose>
              <xsl:when test="$fFunc=1">
                <xsl:call-template name="WriteFunc">
                  <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
                </xsl:call-template>
              </xsl:when>
              <xsl:otherwise>
                <xsl:variable name="fFenced">
                  <xsl:call-template name="FFenced">
                    <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
                  </xsl:call-template>
                </xsl:variable>
                <xsl:choose>
                  <xsl:when test="$fFenced=1">
                    <xsl:call-template name="WriteFenced">
                      <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
                    </xsl:call-template>
                  </xsl:when>
                  <xsl:otherwise>
                    <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*" />
                  </xsl:otherwise>
                </xsl:choose>
              </xsl:otherwise>
            </xsl:choose>
          </xsl:otherwise>
        </xsl:choose>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:mstyle]">
        <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*" />
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:mfrac]">
        <xsl:call-template name="MFrac">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:msub]">
        <xsl:call-template name="MSub">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:msup]">
        <xsl:call-template name="MSup">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:msubsup]">
        <xsl:call-template name="MSubSup">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:mroot]">
        <xsl:call-template name="MRoot">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:menclose] | $ndCur[self::mml:msqrt]">
        <xsl:call-template name="MEncloseMSqrt">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:mfenced]">
        <xsl:call-template name="MFenced">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:mpadded]">
        <xsl:call-template name="MPadded">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:mphantom]">
        <xsl:call-template name="MPhantom">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:munder]">
        <xsl:call-template name="MUnder">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:mover]">
        <xsl:call-template name="MOver">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:munderover]">
        <xsl:call-template name="MUnderOver">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:mmultiscripts]">
        <xsl:call-template name="MMultiscripts">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:mtable]">
        <xsl:call-template name="MTable">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:mi][not(child::mml:mglyph)] |
                      $ndCur[self::mml:mn][not(child::mml:mglyph)] |
                      $ndCur[self::mml:mo][not(child::mml:mglyph)] |
                      $ndCur[self::mml:ms][not(child::mml:mglyph)] |
                      $ndCur[self::mml:mtext][not(child::mml:mglyph)]">
        <xsl:call-template name="MNonGlyphToken">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- MathML munder/mover can represent several Omml constructs 
       (m:bar, m:limLow, m:limUpp, m:acc, m:groupChr, etc.).  The following 
       templates (FIsBar, FIsAcc, and FIsGroupChr) are used to determine 
			 which of these Omml constructs an munder/mover should be translated into. -->

  <!-- Note:  ndCur should only be an munder/mover MathML element.
  
       ndCur should be interpretted as an m:bar if
          1)  its respective accent attribute is not true
          2)  its second child is an mml:mo
          3)  the character of the mml:mo is the correct under/over bar. -->
  <xsl:template name="FIsBar">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" />
    <xsl:variable name="fUnder">
      <xsl:choose>
        <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:munder]">1</xsl:when>
        <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
      </xsl:choose>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:variable name="sLowerCaseAccent">
      <xsl:choose>
        <xsl:when test="$fUnder=1">
          <xsl:choose>
            <xsl:when test="$ndCur/@accentunder">
              <xsl:value-of select="translate($ndCur/@accentunder, $StrUCAlphabet, $StrLCAlphabet)"/>
            </xsl:when>
            <xsl:otherwise>
              <xsl:value-of select="translate($ndCur/@mml:accentunder, $StrUCAlphabet, $StrLCAlphabet)"/>
            </xsl:otherwise>
          </xsl:choose>
        </xsl:when>
        <xsl:otherwise>
          <xsl:choose>
            <xsl:when test="$ndCur/@accent">
              <xsl:value-of select="translate($ndCur/@accent, $StrUCAlphabet, $StrLCAlphabet)"/>
            </xsl:when>
            <xsl:otherwise>
              <xsl:value-of select="translate($ndCur/@mml:accent, $StrUCAlphabet, $StrLCAlphabet)"/>
            </xsl:otherwise>
          </xsl:choose>
        </xsl:otherwise>
      </xsl:choose>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:variable name="fAccent">
      <xsl:choose>
        <xsl:when test="$sLowerCaseAccent='true'">1</xsl:when>
        <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
      </xsl:choose>
    </xsl:variable>

    <xsl:choose>
      <!-- The script is unaccented and the second child is an mo -->
      <xsl:when test="$fAccent = 0 
                      and $ndCur/child::*[2]/self::mml:mo">
        <xsl:variable name="sOperator">
          <xsl:value-of select="$ndCur/child::*[2]" />
        </xsl:variable>
        <xsl:choose>
          <!-- Should we write an underbar? -->
          <xsl:when test="$fUnder = 1">
            <xsl:choose>
              <xsl:when test="$sOperator = '&#x0332;' or $sOperator = '&#x005F;'">1</xsl:when>
              <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
            </xsl:choose>
          </xsl:when>
          <!-- Should we write an overbar? -->
          <xsl:otherwise>
            <xsl:choose>
              <xsl:when test="$sOperator = '&#x0305;' or $sOperator = '&#x00AF;'">1</xsl:when>
              <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
            </xsl:choose>
          </xsl:otherwise>
        </xsl:choose>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>

  <!-- Note:  ndCur should only be an mover MathML element.
  
       ndCur should be interpretted as an m:acc if
          1)  its accent attribute is true
          2)  its second child is an mml:mo
          3)  there is only zero or one character in the mml:mo -->
  <xsl:template name="FIsAcc">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />

    <xsl:variable name="sLowerCaseAccent">
      <xsl:choose>
        <xsl:when test="$ndCur/@accent">
          <xsl:value-of select="translate($ndCur/@accent, $StrUCAlphabet, $StrLCAlphabet)"/>
        </xsl:when>
        <xsl:otherwise>
          <xsl:value-of select="translate($ndCur/@mml:accent, $StrUCAlphabet, $StrLCAlphabet)"/>
        </xsl:otherwise>
      </xsl:choose>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:variable name="sLowerCaseMoAccent">
      <xsl:choose>
        <xsl:when test="$ndCur/child::*[2] = mml:mo and $ndCur/child::*[2]/@accent">
          <xsl:value-of select="translate($ndCur/child::*[2]/@accent, $StrUCAlphabet, $StrLCAlphabet)"/>
        </xsl:when>
        <xsl:when test="$ndCur/child::*[2] = mml:mo and $ndCur/child::*[2]/@mml:accent">
          <xsl:value-of select="translate($ndCur/child::*[2]/@mml:accent, $StrUCAlphabet, $StrLCAlphabet)"/>
        </xsl:when>
      </xsl:choose>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:variable name="fAccent">
      <xsl:choose>
        <xsl:when test="$sLowerCaseMoAccent='true' or ($sLowerCaseMoAccent='' and $sLowerCaseAccent='true')">1</xsl:when>
        <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
      </xsl:choose>
    </xsl:variable>

    <xsl:choose>
      <!-- The script is accented and the second child is an mo -->
      <xsl:when test="$fAccent = 1 
                      and $ndCur/child::*[2] = mml:mo">
        <xsl:variable name="sOperator">
          <xsl:value-of select="$ndCur/child::*[2]" />
        </xsl:variable>
        <xsl:choose>
          <!-- There is only one operator, this is a valid Omml accent! -->
          <xsl:when test="string-length($sOperator) &lt;= 1">1</xsl:when>
          <!-- More than one accented operator.  This isn't a valid
               omml accent -->
          <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
        </xsl:choose>
      </xsl:when>
      <!-- Not accented, not an operator, or both, but in any case, this is
           not an Omml accent. -->
      <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- Is ndCur a groupChr? 
			 ndCur is a groupChr if:
			 
				 1.  The accent is false (note:  accent attribute 
						 for munder is accentunder). 
				 2.  ndCur is an munder or mover.
				 3.  ndCur has two children
				 4.  Of these two children, one is an mml:mo and the other is an mml:mrow
				 5.  The number of characters in the mml:mo is 1.
			 
			 If all of the above are true, then return 1, else return 0.
	-->
  <xsl:template name="FIsGroupChr">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:variable name="fUnder">
      <xsl:choose>
        <xsl:when test="$ndCur[self::mml:munder]">1</xsl:when>
        <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
      </xsl:choose>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:variable name="sLowerCaseAccent">
      <xsl:choose>
        <xsl:when test="$fUnder=1">
          <xsl:choose>
            <xsl:when test="$ndCur/@accentunder">
              <xsl:value-of select="translate($ndCur/@accentunder, $StrUCAlphabet, $StrLCAlphabet)"/>
            </xsl:when>
            <xsl:otherwise>
              <xsl:value-of select="translate($ndCur/@mml:accentunder, $StrUCAlphabet, $StrLCAlphabet)"/>
            </xsl:otherwise>
          </xsl:choose>
        </xsl:when>
        <xsl:otherwise>
          <xsl:choose>
            <xsl:when test="$ndCur/@accent">
              <xsl:value-of select="translate($ndCur/@accent, $StrUCAlphabet, $StrLCAlphabet)"/>
            </xsl:when>
            <xsl:otherwise>
              <xsl:value-of select="translate($ndCur/@mml:accent, $StrUCAlphabet, $StrLCAlphabet)"/>
            </xsl:otherwise>
          </xsl:choose>
        </xsl:otherwise>
      </xsl:choose>
    </xsl:variable>

    <xsl:variable name="fAccentFalse">
      <xsl:choose>
        <xsl:when test="$sLowerCaseAccent='false'">1</xsl:when>
        <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
      </xsl:choose>
    </xsl:variable>

    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="$fAccentFalse=1
                      and $ndCur[self::mml:munder or self::mml:mover] 
                      and count($ndCur/child::*)=2 
                      and (($ndCur/child::*[1][self::mml:mrow] and $ndCur/child::*[2][self::mml:mo]) 
                            or ($ndCur/child::*[1][self::mml:mo] and $ndCur/child::*[2][self::mml:mrow]))">
        <xsl:variable name="sOperator">
          <xsl:value-of select="$ndCur/child::mml:mo" />
        </xsl:variable>
        <xsl:choose>
          <xsl:when test="string-length($sOperator) &lt;= 1">1</xsl:when>
          <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
        </xsl:choose>
      </xsl:when>

      <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- %%Template: match munder
  -->
  <xsl:template match="mml:munder">
    <xsl:variable name="fNaryArgument">
      <xsl:call-template name="FIsNaryArgument">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:if test="$fNaryArgument=0">
      <xsl:call-template name="MUnder">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:if>
  </xsl:template>

  <xsl:template name="MUnder">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:variable name="fNary">
      <xsl:call-template name="isNary">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="$fNary='true'">
        <m:nary>
          <xsl:call-template name="CreateNaryProp">
            <xsl:with-param name="chr">
              <xsl:value-of select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[1])" />
            </xsl:with-param>
            <xsl:with-param name="sMathmlType" select="'munder'" />
          </xsl:call-template>
          <m:sub>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[2]" />
          </m:sub>
          <m:sup>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
          </m:sup>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:call-template name="NaryHandleMrowMstyle">
              <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="($ndCur[parent::*[count(*) &gt; 1]] |
                                                   $ndCur/parent::*[self::mml:mstyle[count(*)=1]])/following-sibling::*[1]" />
            </xsl:call-template>
          </m:e>
        </m:nary>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>
        <!-- Should this munder be interpreted as an OMML m:bar? -->
        <xsl:variable name="fIsBar">
          <xsl:call-template name="FIsBar">
            <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
          </xsl:call-template>
        </xsl:variable>
        <xsl:choose>
          <xsl:when test="$fIsBar=1">
            <m:bar>
              <m:barPr>
                <m:pos m:val="bot" />
              </m:barPr>
              <m:e>
                <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
              </m:e>
            </m:bar>
          </xsl:when>
          <xsl:otherwise>
            <!-- It isn't an integral or underbar, is this a groupChr? -->
            <xsl:variable name="fGroupChr">
              <xsl:call-template name="FIsGroupChr">
                <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
              </xsl:call-template>
            </xsl:variable>
            <xsl:choose>
              <xsl:when test="$fGroupChr=1">
                <xsl:element name="m:groupChr">
                  <xsl:call-template name="CreateGroupChrPr">
                    <xsl:with-param name="chr">
                      <xsl:value-of select="$ndCur/mml:mo" />
                    </xsl:with-param>
                    <xsl:with-param name="pos">
                      <xsl:choose>
                        <xsl:when test="$ndCur/*[1][self::mml:mrow]">bot</xsl:when>
                        <xsl:otherwise>top</xsl:otherwise>
                      </xsl:choose>
                    </xsl:with-param>
                    <xsl:with-param name="vertJc">top</xsl:with-param>
                  </xsl:call-template>
                  <xsl:element name="m:e">
                    <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/mml:mrow" />
                  </xsl:element>
                </xsl:element>
              </xsl:when>
              <xsl:otherwise>
                <!-- Generic munder -->
                <xsl:element name="m:limLow">
                  <xsl:element name="m:e">
                    <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                    <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
                  </xsl:element>
                  <xsl:element name="m:lim">
                    <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                    <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[2]" />
                  </xsl:element>
                </xsl:element>
              </xsl:otherwise>
            </xsl:choose>
          </xsl:otherwise>
        </xsl:choose>
      </xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- Given the values for chr, pos, and vertJc, create an omml
	     groupChr's groupChrPr -->
  <xsl:template name="CreateGroupChrPr">
    <xsl:param name="chr">&#x23df;</xsl:param>
    <xsl:param name="pos" select="bot" />
    <xsl:param name="vertJc" select="top" />
    <xsl:element name="m:groupChrPr">
      <xsl:element name="m:chr">
        <xsl:attribute name="m:val">
          <xsl:value-of select="$chr"/>
        </xsl:attribute>
      </xsl:element>
      <xsl:element name="m:pos">
        <xsl:attribute name="m:val">
          <xsl:value-of select="$pos"/>
        </xsl:attribute>
      </xsl:element>
      <xsl:element name="m:vertJc">
        <xsl:attribute name="m:val">
          <xsl:value-of select="$vertJc"/>
        </xsl:attribute>
      </xsl:element>
    </xsl:element>
  </xsl:template>


  <!--
      Convert a non-combining character into its upper combining
      couterpart.
      
      { Non-combining, Upper-combining }
      {U+02D8, U+0306}, // BREVE
      {U+00B8, U+0312}, // CEDILLA
      {U+0060, U+0300}, // GRAVE ACCENT
      {U+002D, U+0305}, // HYPHEN-MINUS/OVERLINE
      {U+2212, U+0305}, // MINUS SIGN/OVERLINE
      {U+002E, U+0305}, // FULL STOP/DOT ABOVE
      {U+02D9, U+0307}, // DOT ABOVE
      {U+02DD, U+030B}, // DOUBLE ACUTE ACCENT
      {U+00B4, U+0301}, // ACUTE ACCENT
      {U+007E, U+0303}, // TILDE
      {U+02DC, U+0303}, // SMALL TILDE
      {U+00A8, U+0308}, // DIAERESIS
      {U+02C7, U+030C}, // CARON
      {U+005E, U+0302}, // CIRCUMFLEX ACCENT
      {U+00AF, U+0305}, // MACRON
      {U+005F, ::::::}, // LOW LINE
      {U+2192, U+20D7}, // RIGHTWARDS ARROW
      {U+27F6, U+20D7}, // LONG RIGHTWARDS ARROW
      {U+2190, U+20D6}, // LEFT ARROW
  -->
  <xsl:template name="ToUpperCombining">
    <xsl:param name="ch" />
    <xsl:choose>
      <!-- BREVE -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x02D8;'">&#x0306;</xsl:when>
      <!-- CEDILLA -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x00B8;'">&#x0312;</xsl:when>
      <!-- GRAVE ACCENT -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x0060;'">&#x0300;</xsl:when>
      <!-- HYPHEN-MINUS/OVERLINE -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x002D;'">&#x0305;</xsl:when>
      <!-- MINUS SIGN/OVERLINE -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x2212;'">&#x0305;</xsl:when>
      <!-- FULL STOP/DOT ABOVE -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x002E;'">&#x0307;</xsl:when>
      <!-- DOT ABOVE -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x02D9;'">&#x0307;</xsl:when>
      <!-- DOUBLE ACUTE ACCENT -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x02DD;'">&#x030B;</xsl:when>
      <!-- ACUTE ACCENT -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x00B4;'">&#x0301;</xsl:when>
      <!-- TILDE -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x007E;'">&#x0303;</xsl:when>
      <!-- SMALL TILDE -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x02DC;'">&#x0303;</xsl:when>
      <!-- DIAERESIS -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x00A8;'">&#x0308;</xsl:when>
      <!-- CARON -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x02C7;'">&#x030C;</xsl:when>
      <!-- CIRCUMFLEX ACCENT -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x005E;'">&#x0302;</xsl:when>
      <!-- MACRON -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x00AF;'">&#x0305;</xsl:when>
      
      <!-- LOW LINE -->
      
      <!-- RIGHTWARDS ARROW -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x2192;'">&#x20D7;</xsl:when>
      <!-- LONG RIGHTWARDS ARROW -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x27F6;'">&#x20D7;</xsl:when>
      <!-- LEFT ARROW -->
      <xsl:when test="$ch='&#x2190;'">&#x20D6;</xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>
        <xsl:value-of select="$ch"/>
      </xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- %%Template: match mover
  -->
  <xsl:template match="mml:mover">
    <xsl:variable name="fNaryArgument">
      <xsl:call-template name="FIsNaryArgument">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:if test="$fNaryArgument=0">
      <xsl:call-template name="MOver">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:if>
  </xsl:template>

  <xsl:template name="MOver">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:variable name="fNary">
      <xsl:call-template name="isNary">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="$fNary='true'">
        <m:nary>
          <xsl:call-template name="CreateNaryProp">
            <xsl:with-param name="chr">
              <xsl:value-of select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[1])" />
            </xsl:with-param>
            <xsl:with-param name="sMathmlType" select="'mover'" />
          </xsl:call-template>
          <m:sub>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
          </m:sub>
          <m:sup>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[2]" />
          </m:sup>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:call-template name="NaryHandleMrowMstyle">
              <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="($ndCur[parent::*[count(*) > 1]] |
                                                   $ndCur/parent::*[self::mml:mstyle[count(*) = 1]])/following-sibling::*[1]" />
            </xsl:call-template>
          </m:e>
        </m:nary>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>
        <!-- Should this munder be interpreted as an OMML m:bar or m:acc? -->

        <!-- Check to see if this is an m:bar -->
        <xsl:variable name="fIsBar">
          <xsl:call-template name="FIsBar">
            <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
          </xsl:call-template>
        </xsl:variable>
        <xsl:choose>
          <xsl:when test="$fIsBar = 1">
            <m:bar>
              <m:barPr>
                <m:pos m:val="top" />
              </m:barPr>
              <m:e>
                <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
              </m:e>
            </m:bar>
          </xsl:when>
          <xsl:otherwise>
            <!-- Not an m:bar, should it be an m:acc? -->
            <xsl:variable name="fIsAcc">
              <xsl:call-template name="FIsAcc">
                <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
              </xsl:call-template>
            </xsl:variable>
            <xsl:choose>
              <xsl:when test="$fIsAcc=1">
                <m:acc>
                  <m:accPr>
                    <m:chr>
                      <xsl:variable name="ch">
                        <xsl:value-of select="$ndCur/*[2]" />
                      </xsl:variable>
                      <xsl:variable name="chComb">
                        <xsl:call-template name="ToUpperCombining">
                          <xsl:with-param name="ch" select="$ch" />
                        </xsl:call-template>
                      </xsl:variable>
                      <xsl:attribute name="m:val">
                        <xsl:value-of select="$chComb" />
                      </xsl:attribute>
                    </m:chr>
                  </m:accPr>
                  <m:e>
                    <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                    <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
                  </m:e>
                </m:acc>
              </xsl:when>
              <xsl:otherwise>
                <!-- This isn't an integral, overbar or accent, 
								     could it be a groupChr? -->
                <xsl:variable name="fGroupChr">
                  <xsl:call-template name="FIsGroupChr">
                    <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
                  </xsl:call-template>
                </xsl:variable>
                <xsl:choose>
                  <xsl:when test="$fGroupChr=1">
                    <xsl:element name="m:groupChr">
                      <xsl:call-template name="CreateGroupChrPr">
                        <xsl:with-param name="chr">
                          <xsl:value-of select="$ndCur/mml:mo" />
                        </xsl:with-param>
                        <xsl:with-param name="pos">
                          <xsl:choose>
                            <xsl:when test="$ndCur/*[1][self::mml:mrow]">top</xsl:when>
                            <xsl:otherwise>bot</xsl:otherwise>
                          </xsl:choose>
                        </xsl:with-param>
                        <xsl:with-param name="vertJc">bot</xsl:with-param>
                      </xsl:call-template>
                      <xsl:element name="m:e">
                        <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/mml:mrow" />
                      </xsl:element>
                    </xsl:element>
                  </xsl:when>
                  <xsl:otherwise>
                    <!-- Generic mover -->
                    <xsl:element name="m:limUpp">
                      <xsl:element name="m:e">
                        <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                        <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
                      </xsl:element>
                      <xsl:element name="m:lim">
                        <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                        <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[2]" />
                      </xsl:element>
                    </xsl:element>
                  </xsl:otherwise>
                </xsl:choose>
              </xsl:otherwise>
            </xsl:choose>
          </xsl:otherwise>
        </xsl:choose>
      </xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- %%Template: match munderover
  -->
  <xsl:template match="mml:munderover">
    <xsl:variable name="fNaryArgument">
      <xsl:call-template name="FIsNaryArgument">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:if test="$fNaryArgument=0">
      <xsl:call-template name="MUnderOver">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:if>
  </xsl:template>

  <xsl:template name="MUnderOver">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:variable name="fNary">
      <xsl:call-template name="isNary">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="$fNary='true'">
        <m:nary>
          <xsl:call-template name="CreateNaryProp">
            <xsl:with-param name="chr">
              <xsl:value-of select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[1])" />
            </xsl:with-param>
            <xsl:with-param name="sMathmlType" select="'munderover'" />
          </xsl:call-template>
          <m:sub>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[2]" />
          </m:sub>
          <m:sup>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[3]" />
          </m:sup>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:call-template name="NaryHandleMrowMstyle">
              <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="($ndCur[parent::*[count(*) > 1]] |
                                                   $ndCur/parent::*[self::mml:mstyle[count(*) = 1]])/following-sibling::*[1]" />
            </xsl:call-template>
          </m:e>
        </m:nary>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>
        <xsl:element name="m:limUpp">
          <xsl:element name="m:e">
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:element name="m:limLow">
              <xsl:element name="m:e">
                <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
              </xsl:element>
              <xsl:element name="m:lim">
                <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[2]" />
              </xsl:element>
            </xsl:element>
          </xsl:element>
          <xsl:element name="m:lim">
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[3]" />
          </xsl:element>
        </xsl:element>
      </xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>

  <!-- %%Template: match mfenced
  -->
  <xsl:template match="mml:mfenced">
    <xsl:variable name="fNaryArgument">
      <xsl:call-template name="FIsNaryArgument">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:if test="$fNaryArgument=0">
      <xsl:call-template name="MFenced">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:if>
  </xsl:template>

  <xsl:template name="MFenced">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <m:d>
      <xsl:call-template name="CreateDelimProp">
        <xsl:with-param name="fChOpenValid">
          <xsl:choose>
            <xsl:when test="$ndCur/@open">
              <xsl:value-of select="1" />
            </xsl:when>
            <xsl:when test="$ndCur/@mml:open">
              <xsl:value-of select="1" />
            </xsl:when>
            <xsl:otherwise>
              <xsl:value-of select="0" />
            </xsl:otherwise>
          </xsl:choose>
        </xsl:with-param>
        <xsl:with-param name="chOpen">
          <xsl:choose>
            <xsl:when test="$ndCur/@open">
              <xsl:value-of select="$ndCur/@open" />
            </xsl:when>
            <xsl:otherwise>
              <xsl:value-of select="$ndCur/@mml:open" />
            </xsl:otherwise>
          </xsl:choose>
        </xsl:with-param>
        <xsl:with-param name="fChSeparatorsValid">
          <xsl:choose>
            <xsl:when test="$ndCur/@separators">
              <xsl:value-of select="1" />
            </xsl:when>
            <xsl:when test="$ndCur/@mml:separators">
              <xsl:value-of select="1" />
            </xsl:when>
            <xsl:otherwise>
              <xsl:value-of select="0" />
            </xsl:otherwise>
          </xsl:choose>
        </xsl:with-param>
        <xsl:with-param name="chSeparators">
          <xsl:choose>
            <xsl:when test="$ndCur/@separators">
              <xsl:value-of select="$ndCur/@separators" />
            </xsl:when>
            <xsl:otherwise>
              <xsl:value-of select="$ndCur/@mml:separators" />
            </xsl:otherwise>
          </xsl:choose>
        </xsl:with-param>
        <xsl:with-param name="fChCloseValid">
          <xsl:choose>
            <xsl:when test="$ndCur/@close">
              <xsl:value-of select="1" />
            </xsl:when>
            <xsl:when test="$ndCur/@mml:close">
              <xsl:value-of select="1" />
            </xsl:when>
            <xsl:otherwise>
              <xsl:value-of select="0" />
            </xsl:otherwise>
          </xsl:choose>
        </xsl:with-param>
        <xsl:with-param name="chClose">
          <xsl:choose>
            <xsl:when test="$ndCur/@close">
              <xsl:value-of select="$ndCur/@close" />
            </xsl:when>
            <xsl:otherwise>
              <xsl:value-of select="$ndCur/@mml:close" />
            </xsl:otherwise>
          </xsl:choose>
        </xsl:with-param>
      </xsl:call-template>
      <xsl:for-each select="$ndCur/*">
        <m:e>
          <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
          <xsl:apply-templates select="." />
        </m:e>
      </xsl:for-each>
    </m:d>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- %%Template: writes an m:d tag for fenced structure that is formed by an mrow with
       an mo with a fence open character as the first child and/or an mo with a fence close
       character as the last child. If the middle mo elements all use the same character,
       then that character will be used as the separator character.
  -->
  <xsl:template name="WriteFenced">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />

    <xsl:variable name="chOpen">
      <xsl:choose>
        <xsl:when test="$ndCur/*[1][self::mml:mo]">
          <xsl:value-of select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[1])" />
        </xsl:when>
        <xsl:when test="$ndCur/*[1][self::mml:mrow[count(*)=1 and mml:mo]]">
          <xsl:value-of select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[1]/mml:mo)" />
        </xsl:when>
        <xsl:otherwise>
          <xsl:value-of select="''" />
        </xsl:otherwise>
      </xsl:choose>
    </xsl:variable>

    <xsl:variable name="chClose">
      <xsl:choose>
        <xsl:when test="$ndCur/*[last()][self::mml:mo]">
          <xsl:value-of select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[last()])" />
        </xsl:when>
        <xsl:when test="$ndCur/*[last()][self::mml:mrow[count(*)=1 and mml:mo]]">
          <xsl:value-of select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[last()]/mml:mo)" />
        </xsl:when>
        <xsl:otherwise>
          <xsl:value-of select="''" />
        </xsl:otherwise>
      </xsl:choose>
    </xsl:variable>

    <xsl:variable name="fenceSeparatorChar">
      <xsl:call-template name="FenceSeparatorChar">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <m:d>
      <xsl:call-template name="CreateDelimProp">
        <xsl:with-param name="fChOpenValid">
          <xsl:value-of select="1" />
        </xsl:with-param>
        <xsl:with-param name="chOpen">
          <xsl:value-of select="$chOpen" />
        </xsl:with-param>
        <xsl:with-param name="fChSeparatorsValid">
          <xsl:value-of select="1" />
        </xsl:with-param>
        <xsl:with-param name="chSeparators">
          <xsl:value-of select="$fenceSeparatorChar" />
        </xsl:with-param>
        <xsl:with-param name="fChCloseValid">
          <xsl:value-of select="1" />
        </xsl:with-param>
        <xsl:with-param name="chClose">
          <xsl:value-of select="$chClose" />
        </xsl:with-param>
      </xsl:call-template>

      <!-- Write an m:e element containing everything from the beginning
           until the first fence separator if there is one, excluding the
           fence open and fence close if there are any. -->
      <m:e>
        <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
        <xsl:for-each select="$ndCur/*[1][not(self::mml:mo[normalize-space(.) = $chOpen])
                                         and not(self::mml:mrow[count(*)=1 and mml:mo[normalize-space(.) = $chOpen]])] |
          $ndCur/*[position() &gt; 1][
            not((. | preceding-sibling::*)
            [self::mml:mo
              [normalize-space(.) = $fenceSeparatorChar]
            ]) and
            not(position() = last() and (self::mml:mo[normalize-space(.) = $chClose]
              or self::mml:mrow[count(*)=1 and mml:mo[normalize-space(.) = $chClose]]))
          ]">
          <xsl:apply-templates select="." />
        </xsl:for-each>
      </m:e>

      <!-- Write additional m:e elements for each fence separator containing
           everything from that fence separator until the next fence separator
           if there is one or the fence close if there is one. -->
      <xsl:for-each select="$ndCur/*[position() &gt; 1 and position() &lt; last()][self::mml:mo][normalize-space(.)=$fenceSeparatorChar]">
        <xsl:variable name="separatorIndex">
          <xsl:value-of select="position()" />
        </xsl:variable>
        <m:e>
          <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
          <xsl:for-each select="
            following-sibling::*[
              count((. | preceding-sibling::*)
              [self::mml:mo
                [normalize-space(.) = $fenceSeparatorChar]
              ]) = $separatorIndex
            ][not(position() = last() and normalize-space(.) = $chClose)]">
            <xsl:apply-templates select="." />
          </xsl:for-each>
        </m:e>
      </xsl:for-each>
    </m:d>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- %%Template: Helper to write the fenced m:d element for WriteFencedWithScript.
       The content includes all children of ndCur between the first child (open fence)
       and the last child (script element containing the close fence as its base).
  -->
  <xsl:template name="WriteFencedContent">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:param name="chOpen" />
    <xsl:param name="chClose" />
    <m:d>
      <xsl:call-template name="CreateDelimProp">
        <xsl:with-param name="fChOpenValid" select="1" />
        <xsl:with-param name="chOpen" select="$chOpen" />
        <xsl:with-param name="fChSeparatorsValid" select="1" />
        <xsl:with-param name="chSeparators" select="''" />
        <xsl:with-param name="fChCloseValid" select="1" />
        <xsl:with-param name="chClose" select="$chClose" />
      </xsl:call-template>
      <m:e>
        <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
        <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[position() &gt; 1 and position() &lt; last()]" />
      </m:e>
    </m:d>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- %%Template: writes the appropriate script structure (sSup, sSub, sSubSup, limLow,
       limUpp) with an m:d (fenced) expression as the base. This handles the case where
       MathML has a fenced expression where the closing fence character is inside a script
       element as its base.
       For example, KaTeX generates (x)^2 as:
         <mrow><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><msup><mo>)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow>
       This template produces:
         <m:sSup><m:e><m:d>...(x)...</m:d></m:e><m:sup>2</m:sup></m:sSup>
  -->
  <xsl:template name="WriteFencedWithScript">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:variable name="chOpen" select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[1])" />
    <xsl:variable name="chClose" select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[last()]/*[1])" />
    <xsl:variable name="ndScript" select="$ndCur/*[last()]" />

    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="$ndScript[self::mml:msup]">
        <m:sSup>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:call-template name="WriteFencedContent">
              <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
              <xsl:with-param name="chOpen" select="$chOpen" />
              <xsl:with-param name="chClose" select="$chClose" />
            </xsl:call-template>
          </m:e>
          <m:sup>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndScript/*[2]" />
          </m:sup>
        </m:sSup>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndScript[self::mml:msub]">
        <m:sSub>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:call-template name="WriteFencedContent">
              <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
              <xsl:with-param name="chOpen" select="$chOpen" />
              <xsl:with-param name="chClose" select="$chClose" />
            </xsl:call-template>
          </m:e>
          <m:sub>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndScript/*[2]" />
          </m:sub>
        </m:sSub>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndScript[self::mml:msubsup]">
        <m:sSubSup>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:call-template name="WriteFencedContent">
              <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
              <xsl:with-param name="chOpen" select="$chOpen" />
              <xsl:with-param name="chClose" select="$chClose" />
            </xsl:call-template>
          </m:e>
          <m:sub>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndScript/*[2]" />
          </m:sub>
          <m:sup>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndScript/*[3]" />
          </m:sup>
        </m:sSubSup>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndScript[self::mml:munder]">
        <m:limLow>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:call-template name="WriteFencedContent">
              <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
              <xsl:with-param name="chOpen" select="$chOpen" />
              <xsl:with-param name="chClose" select="$chClose" />
            </xsl:call-template>
          </m:e>
          <m:lim>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndScript/*[2]" />
          </m:lim>
        </m:limLow>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndScript[self::mml:mover]">
        <m:limUpp>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:call-template name="WriteFencedContent">
              <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
              <xsl:with-param name="chOpen" select="$chOpen" />
              <xsl:with-param name="chClose" select="$chClose" />
            </xsl:call-template>
          </m:e>
          <m:lim>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndScript/*[2]" />
          </m:lim>
        </m:limUpp>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="$ndScript[self::mml:munderover]">
        <m:limUpp>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <m:limLow>
              <m:e>
                <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                <xsl:call-template name="WriteFencedContent">
                  <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
                  <xsl:with-param name="chOpen" select="$chOpen" />
                  <xsl:with-param name="chClose" select="$chClose" />
                </xsl:call-template>
              </m:e>
              <m:lim>
                <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndScript/*[2]" />
              </m:lim>
            </m:limLow>
          </m:e>
          <m:lim>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndScript/*[3]" />
          </m:lim>
        </m:limUpp>
      </xsl:when>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>


  <!-- %%Template: CreateDelimProp
	
		Given the characters to use as open, close and separators for 
		the delim object, create the m:dPr (delim properties). 		
		
		MathML can have any number of separators in an mfenced object, but 
		OMML can only represent one separator for each d (delim) object.
		So, we pick the first separator specified. 		
	-->
  <xsl:template name="CreateDelimProp">
    <xsl:param name="fChOpenValid" />
    <xsl:param name="chOpen" />
    <xsl:param name="fChSeparatorsValid" />
    <xsl:param name="chSeparators" />
    <xsl:param name="fChCloseValid" />
    <xsl:param name="chClose" />
    <xsl:variable name="chSep" select="substring($chSeparators, 1, 1)" />

    <!-- do we need a dPr at all? If everything's at its default value, then 
			don't bother at all -->
    <xsl:if test="($fChOpenValid=1 and not($chOpen = '(')) or
						  ($fChCloseValid=1 and not($chClose = ')')) or 
						  not($chSep = '|')">
      <m:dPr>
        <!-- the default for MathML and OMML is '('. -->
        <xsl:if test="$fChOpenValid=1 and not($chOpen = '(')">
          <m:begChr>
            <xsl:attribute name="m:val">
              <xsl:value-of select="$chOpen" />
            </xsl:attribute>
          </m:begChr>
        </xsl:if>

        <!-- the default for MathML is ',' and for OMML is '|' -->

        <xsl:choose>
          <!-- matches OMML's default, don't bother to write anything out -->
          <xsl:when test="$chSep = '|'" />

          <!-- Not specified, use MathML's default. We test against 
					the existence of the actual attribute, not the substring -->
          <xsl:when test="$fChSeparatorsValid=0">
            <m:sepChr m:val=',' />
          </xsl:when>

          <xsl:otherwise>
            <m:sepChr>
              <xsl:attribute name="m:val">
                <xsl:value-of select="$chSep" />
              </xsl:attribute>
            </m:sepChr>
          </xsl:otherwise>
        </xsl:choose>

        <!-- the default for MathML and OMML is ')'. -->
        <xsl:if test="$fChCloseValid=1 and not($chClose = ')')">
          <m:endChr>
            <xsl:attribute name="m:val">
              <xsl:value-of select="$chClose" />
            </xsl:attribute>
          </m:endChr>
        </xsl:if>
      </m:dPr>
    </xsl:if>
  </xsl:template>

  <xsl:template name="LQuoteFromMs">
    <xsl:param name="msCur" select="." />
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="(not($msCur/@lquote) or $msCur/@lquote='')
                      and (not($msCur/@mml:lquote) or $msCur/@mml:lquote='')">
        <xsl:text>"</xsl:text>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>
        <xsl:choose>
          <xsl:when test="$msCur/@lquote">
            <xsl:value-of select="$msCur/@lquote"/>
          </xsl:when>
          <xsl:otherwise>
            <xsl:value-of select="$msCur/@mml:lquote"/>
          </xsl:otherwise>
        </xsl:choose>
      </xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>

  <xsl:template name="RQuoteFromMs">
    <xsl:param name="msCur" select="." />
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="(not($msCur/@rquote) or $msCur/@rquote='')
                       and (not($msCur/@mml:rquote) or $msCur/@mml:rquote='')">
        <xsl:text>"</xsl:text>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>
        <xsl:choose>
          <xsl:when test="$msCur/@rquote">
            <xsl:value-of select="$msCur/@rquote"/>
          </xsl:when>
          <xsl:otherwise>
            <xsl:value-of select="$msCur/@mml:rquote"/>
          </xsl:otherwise>
        </xsl:choose>
      </xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>

  <!-- %%Template: OutputMs
	-->
  <xsl:template name="OutputMs">
    <xsl:param name="msCur" />

    <xsl:variable name="chLquote">
      <xsl:call-template name="LQuoteFromMs">
        <xsl:with-param name="msCur" select="$msCur" />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>

    <xsl:variable name="chRquote">
      <xsl:call-template name="RQuoteFromMs">
        <xsl:with-param name="msCur" select="$msCur" />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>

    <xsl:value-of select="$chLquote"/>
    <xsl:value-of select="normalize-space($msCur)" />
    <xsl:value-of select="$chRquote"/>
  </xsl:template>

  <!-- %%Template: match msub
	-->
  <xsl:template match="mml:msub">
    <xsl:variable name="fNaryArgument">
      <xsl:call-template name="FIsNaryArgument">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:if test="$fNaryArgument=0">
      <xsl:call-template name="MSub">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:if>
  </xsl:template>

  <xsl:template name="MSub">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:variable name="fNary">
      <xsl:call-template name="isNary">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="$fNary='true'">
        <m:nary>
          <xsl:call-template name="CreateNaryProp">
            <xsl:with-param name="chr">
              <xsl:value-of select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[1])" />
            </xsl:with-param>
            <xsl:with-param name="sMathmlType" select="'msub'" />
          </xsl:call-template>
          <m:sub>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[2]" />
          </m:sub>
          <m:sup>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
          </m:sup>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:call-template name="NaryHandleMrowMstyle">
              <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="($ndCur[parent::*[count(*) > 1]] |
                                                   $ndCur/parent::*[self::mml:mstyle[count(*) = 1]])/following-sibling::*[1]" />
            </xsl:call-template>
          </m:e>
        </m:nary>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>
        <m:sSub>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
          </m:e>
          <m:sub>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[2]" />
          </m:sub>
        </m:sSub>
      </xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>

  <!-- %%Template: match msup
	-->
  <xsl:template match="mml:msup">
    <xsl:variable name="fNaryArgument">
      <xsl:call-template name="FIsNaryArgument">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:if test="$fNaryArgument=0">
      <xsl:call-template name="MSup">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:if>
  </xsl:template>

  <xsl:template name="MSup">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:variable name="fNary">
      <xsl:call-template name="isNary">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="$fNary='true'">
        <m:nary>
          <xsl:call-template name="CreateNaryProp">
            <xsl:with-param name="chr">
              <xsl:value-of select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[1])" />
            </xsl:with-param>
            <xsl:with-param name="sMathmlType" select="'msup'" />
          </xsl:call-template>
          <m:sub>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
          </m:sub>
          <m:sup>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[2]" />
          </m:sup>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:call-template name="NaryHandleMrowMstyle">
              <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="($ndCur[parent::*[count(*) > 1]] |
                                                   $ndCur/parent::*[self::mml:mstyle[count(*) = 1]])/following-sibling::*[1]" />
            </xsl:call-template>
          </m:e>
        </m:nary>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>
        <m:sSup>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
          </m:e>
          <m:sup>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[2]" />
          </m:sup>
        </m:sSup>
      </xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>

  <!-- %%Template: match msubsup
	-->
  <xsl:template match="mml:msubsup">
    <xsl:variable name="fNaryArgument">
      <xsl:call-template name="FIsNaryArgument">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:if test="$fNaryArgument=0">
      <xsl:call-template name="MSubSup">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:if>
  </xsl:template>

  <xsl:template name="MSubSup">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:variable name="fNary">
      <xsl:call-template name="isNary">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="$fNary='true'">
        <m:nary>
          <xsl:call-template name="CreateNaryProp">
            <xsl:with-param name="chr">
              <xsl:value-of select="normalize-space($ndCur/*[1])" />
            </xsl:with-param>
            <xsl:with-param name="sMathmlType" select="'msubsup'" />
          </xsl:call-template>
          <m:sub>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[2]" />
          </m:sub>
          <m:sup>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[3]" />
          </m:sup>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:call-template name="NaryHandleMrowMstyle">
              <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="($ndCur[parent::*[count(*) > 1]] |
                                                   $ndCur/parent::*[self::mml:mstyle[count(*) = 1]])/following-sibling::*[1]" />
            </xsl:call-template>
          </m:e>
        </m:nary>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>
        <m:sSubSup>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
          </m:e>
          <m:sub>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[2]" />
          </m:sub>
          <m:sup>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[3]" />
          </m:sup>
        </m:sSubSup>
      </xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>

  <!-- %%Template: SplitScripts 
	
		Takes an collection of nodes, and splits them
		odd and even into sup and sub scripts. Used for dealing with
		mmultiscript.
		
		This template assumes you want to output both a sub and sup element.
		-->
  <xsl:template name="SplitScripts">
    <xsl:param name="ndScripts" />
    <m:sub>
      <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
      <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndScripts[(position() mod 2) = 1]" />
    </m:sub>
    <m:sup>
      <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
      <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndScripts[(position() mod 2) = 0]" />
    </m:sup>
  </xsl:template>

  <!-- %%Template: match mmultiscripts
	
		There is some subtlety with the mml:mprescripts element. Everything that comes before 
		that is considered a script (as opposed to a pre-script), but it need not be present.
	-->
  <xsl:template match="mml:mmultiscripts">
    <xsl:variable name="fNaryArgument">
      <xsl:call-template name="FIsNaryArgument">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>
    <xsl:if test="$fNaryArgument=0">
      <xsl:call-template name="MMultiscripts">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="." />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:if>
  </xsl:template>

  <xsl:template name="MMultiscripts">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />

    <!-- count the nodes. Everything that comes after a mml:mprescripts is considered a pre-script;
			Everything that does not have an mml:mprescript as a preceding-sibling (and is not itself 
			mml:mprescript) is a script, except for the first child which is always the base.
			The mml:none element is a place holder for a sub/sup element slot.
			
			mmultisript pattern:
			<mmultiscript>
				(base)
				(sub sup)* // Where <none/> can replace a sub/sup entry to preserve pattern.
				<mprescripts />
				(presub presup)*
			</mmultiscript>
			-->
    <!-- Count of presecript nodes that we'd print (this is essentially anything but the none placeholder. -->
    <xsl:variable name="cndPrescriptStrict" select="count($ndCur/mml:mprescripts[1]/following-sibling::*[not(self::mml:none)])" />
    <!-- Count of all super script excluding mml:none -->
    <xsl:variable name="cndSuperScript" select="count($ndCur/*[not(preceding-sibling::mml:mprescripts) and
                                                not(self::mml:mprescripts) and
                                                ((position() mod 2) = 1) and
                                                not(self::mml:none)]) - 1" />
    <!-- Count of all sup script excluding mml:none -->
    <xsl:variable name="cndSubScript" select="count($ndCur/*[not(preceding-sibling::mml:mprescripts) and
                                              not(self::mml:mprescripts) and
                                              ((position() mod 2) = 0) and
                                              not(self::mml:none)])" />
    <!-- Count of all scripts excluding mml:none -->
    <xsl:variable name="cndScriptStrict" select="$cndSuperScript + $cndSubScript" />
    <!-- Count of all scripts including mml:none.  This is essentially all nodes before the 
         first mml:mprescripts except the base. -->
    <xsl:variable name="cndScript" select="count($ndCur/*[not(preceding-sibling::mml:mprescripts) and not(self::mml:mprescripts)]) - 1" />

    <xsl:choose>
      <!-- The easy case first. No prescripts, and no script ... just a base -->
      <xsl:when test="$cndPrescriptStrict &lt;= 0 and $cndScriptStrict &lt;= 0">
        <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
      </xsl:when>

      <!-- Next, if there are no prescripts -->
      <xsl:when test="$cndPrescriptStrict &lt;= 0">
        <!-- we know we have some scripts or else we would have taken the earlier
             branch. -->
        <xsl:choose>
          <!-- We have both sub and super scripts-->
          <xsl:when test="$cndSuperScript &gt; 0 and $cndSubScript &gt; 0">
            <m:sSubSup>
              <m:e>
                <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
              </m:e>

              <!-- Every child except the first is a script.  Do the split -->
              <xsl:call-template name="SplitScripts">
                <xsl:with-param name="ndScripts" select="$ndCur/*[position() &gt; 1]" />
              </xsl:call-template>
            </m:sSubSup>
          </xsl:when>
          <!-- Just a sub script -->
          <xsl:when test="$cndSubScript &gt; 0">
            <m:sSub>
              <m:e>
                <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
              </m:e>

              <!-- No prescripts and no super scripts, therefore, it's a sub. -->
              <m:sub>
                <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[position() &gt; 1]" />
              </m:sub>
            </m:sSub>
          </xsl:when>
          <!-- Just super script -->
          <xsl:otherwise>
            <m:sSup>
              <m:e>
                <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
              </m:e>

              <!-- No prescripts and no sub scripts, therefore, it's a sup. -->
              <m:sup>
                <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[position() &gt; 1]" />
              </m:sup>
            </m:sSup>
          </xsl:otherwise>
        </xsl:choose>
      </xsl:when>

      <!-- Next, if there are no scripts -->
      <xsl:when test="$cndScriptStrict &lt;= 0">
        <!-- we know we have some prescripts or else we would have taken the earlier
					  branch. So, create an sPre and split the elements -->
        <m:sPre>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
            <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
          </m:e>

          <!-- The prescripts come after the mml:mprescript and if we get here
							we know there exists some elements after the mml:mprescript element. 
							
							The prescript element has no sub/subsup variation, therefore, even if
							we're only writing sub, we need to write out both the sub and sup element.
							-->
          <xsl:call-template name="SplitScripts">
            <xsl:with-param name="ndScripts" select="$ndCur/mml:mprescripts[1]/following-sibling::*" />
          </xsl:call-template>
        </m:sPre>
      </xsl:when>

      <!-- Finally, the case with both prescripts and scripts. Create an sPre 
				element to house the prescripts, with a sub/sup/subsup element at its base. -->
      <xsl:otherwise>
        <m:sPre>
          <m:e>
            <xsl:choose>
              <!-- We have both sub and super scripts-->
              <xsl:when test="$cndSuperScript &gt; 0 and $cndSubScript &gt; 0">
                <m:sSubSup>
                  <m:e>
                    <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                    <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
                  </m:e>

                  <!-- scripts come before the mml:mprescript but after the first child, so their
								 positions will be 2, 3, ... ($nndScript + 1) -->
                  <xsl:call-template name="SplitScripts">
                    <xsl:with-param name="ndScripts" select="$ndCur/*[(position() &gt; 1) and (position() &lt;= ($cndScript + 1))]" />
                  </xsl:call-template>
                </m:sSubSup>
              </xsl:when>
              <!-- Just a sub script -->
              <xsl:when test="$cndSubScript &gt; 0">
                <m:sSub>
                  <m:e>
                    <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                    <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
                  </m:e>

                  <!-- We have prescripts but no super scripts, therefore, do a sub 
									and apply templates to all tokens counted by cndScript. -->
                  <m:sub>
                    <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[position() &gt; 1 and (position() &lt;= ($cndScript + 1))]" />
                  </m:sub>
                </m:sSub>
              </xsl:when>
              <!-- Just super script -->
              <xsl:otherwise>
                <m:sSup>
                  <m:e>
                    <xsl:call-template name="CreateArgProp" />
                    <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[1]" />
                  </m:e>

                  <!-- We have prescripts but no sub scripts, therefore, do a sub 
									and apply templates to all tokens counted by cndScript. -->
                  <m:sup>
                    <xsl:apply-templates select="$ndCur/*[position() &gt; 1 and (position() &lt;= ($cndScript + 1))]" />
                  </m:sup>
                </m:sSup>
              </xsl:otherwise>
            </xsl:choose>
          </m:e>

          <!-- The prescripts come after the mml:mprescript and if we get here
							we know there exists one such element -->
          <xsl:call-template name="SplitScripts">
            <xsl:with-param name="ndScripts" select="$ndCur/mml:mprescripts[1]/following-sibling::*" />
          </xsl:call-template>
        </m:sPre>
      </xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>

  <!-- Template that determines if ndCur is an equation array.
				
			 ndCur is an equation array if:
			 
			 1.  There are are no frame lines
			 2.  There are no column lines
			 3.  There are no row lines
			 4.  There is no row with more than 1 column  
			 5.  There is no row with fewer than 1 column
			 6.  There are no labeled rows.
			 
	-->
  <xsl:template name="FIsEqArray">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />

    <!-- There should be no frame, columnlines, or rowlines -->
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="(not($ndCur/@frame) or $ndCur/@frame='' or $ndCur/@frame='none')
                      and (not($ndCur/@mml:frame) or $ndCur/@mml:frame='' or $ndCur/@mml:frame='none')
								      and (not($ndCur/@columnlines) or $ndCur/@columnlines='' or $ndCur/@columnlines='none')
                      and (not($ndCur/@mml:columnlines) or $ndCur/@mml:columnlines='' or $ndCur/@mml:columnlines='none')
								      and (not($ndCur/@rowlines) or $ndCur/@rowlines='' or $ndCur/@rowlines='none')
                      and (not($ndCur/@mml:rowlines) or $ndCur/@mml:rowlines='' or $ndCur/@mml:rowlines='none')
								      and not($ndCur/mml:mtr[count(mml:mtd) &gt; 1])
											and not($ndCur/mml:mtr[count(mml:mtd) &lt; 1])
								      and not($ndCur/mml:mlabeledtr)">1</xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>

  <!-- Template used to determine if we should ignore a collection when iterating through 
	     a mathml equation array row.
	
			 So far, the only thing that needs to be ignored is the argument of an nary.  We
			 can ignore this since it is output when we apply-templates to the munder[over]/msub[sup].
	-->
  <xsl:template name="FIgnoreCollection">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />

    <xsl:variable name="fNaryArgument">
      <xsl:call-template name="FIsNaryArgument">
        <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur" />
      </xsl:call-template>
    </xsl:variable>

    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="$fNaryArgument=1">1</xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>

  <!-- Template used to determine if we've already encountered an maligngroup or malignmark.
	
			 This is needed because omml has an implicit spacing alignment (omml spacing alignment = 
			 mathml's maligngroup element) at the beginning of each equation array row.  Therefore, 
			 the first maligngroup (implied or explicit) we encounter does not need to be output.  
			 This template recursively searches up the xml tree and looks at previous siblings to see 
			 if they have a descendant that is an maligngroup or malignmark.  We look for the malignmark 
			 to find the implicit maligngroup.
	-->
  <xsl:template name="FFirstAlignAlreadyFound">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />

    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="count($ndCur/preceding-sibling::*[descendant-or-self::mml:maligngroup
								                                        or descendant-or-self::mml:malignmark]) &gt; 0">1</xsl:when>
      <xsl:when test="not($ndCur/parent::mml:mtd)">
        <xsl:call-template name="FFirstAlignAlreadyFound">
          <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur/parent::*" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>0</xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>

  <!-- This template builds a string that is result of concatenating a given string several times. 
	
			 Given strToRepeat, create a string that has strToRepeat repeated iRepitions times. 
	-->
  <xsl:template name="ConcatStringRepeat">
    <xsl:param name="strToRepeat" select="''" />
    <xsl:param name="iRepetitions" select="0" />
    <xsl:param name="strBuilding" select="''" />

    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="$iRepetitions &lt;= 0">
        <xsl:value-of select="$strBuilding" />
      </xsl:when>
      <xsl:otherwise>
        <xsl:call-template name="ConcatStringRepeat">
          <xsl:with-param name="strToRepeat" select="$strToRepeat" />
          <xsl:with-param name="iRepetitions" select="$iRepetitions - 1" />
          <xsl:with-param name="strBuilding" select="concat($strBuilding, $strToRepeat)" />
        </xsl:call-template>
      </xsl:otherwise>
    </xsl:choose>
  </xsl:template>

  <!-- This template determines if ndCur is a special collection.
			 By special collection, I mean is ndCur the outer element of some special grouping 
			 of mathml elements that actually represents some over all omml structure.
			 
			 For instance, is ndCur a linear fraction, or an omml function.
	-->
  <xsl:template name="FSpecialCollection">
    <xsl:param name="ndCur" select="." />
    <xsl:choose>
      <xsl:when test="$ndCur/self::mml:mrow">
        <xsl:variable name="fLinearFraction">
          <xsl:call-template name="FLinearFrac">
            <xsl:with-param name="ndCur" select="$ndCur"/>
          </xsl:call-template>
